# 2027 New-Grad Timeline

Checked 2026-10-07. 1821 companies in `COMPANY_LIST.md`. Full data: `status.csv` (one row per company) and `postings.csv` (one row per open posting).

- **Apply now:** 917 open postings not yet applied to, at 426 companies: 524 in your areas, 393 elsewhere (137 with a stated deadline)
- **Referrals / cold messages now:** 212 expected to open by 2026-12-02 or overdue vs last year
- **Tracker leads to confirm:** 0 listed by a GitHub tracker, company board not checked yet
- **Later / unknown timing:** 208
- **Companies in TRACKER.md:** 47 (49 applications); other open postings at these companies still appear under Apply now
- **No US new-grad SWE track found:** 860
- **Dropped (citizenship, green card, or clearance required):** 93 — kept in `status.csv` as `excluded`

"Apply now" only counts postings seen on the company's own job board. GitHub trackers (SimplifyJobs, speedyapply, vanshb03) are used for leads and last-cycle dates. Workday dates are the latest repost.
